# Chinese 05 — Band 5–6-oriented lexical and discourse development

This is the text submission for [execution #35](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/35),
implementing [Chinese plan #3, Stage 05](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/3#stage-05)
and [roadmap #1](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/1). The authoritative
[Chinese guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md)
is unchanged at commit `e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f`, blob
`fae450427c762bfe58e8422478323434a71f028b`. Conceptual deck `deck:zh-Hans-CN:05`.

There are exactly **1,000 reviewed source cards and synchronized mappings**,
`zh-05-0001`–`zh-05-1000`, all **P: standard spoken Chinese (Putonghua), Simplified
production**, with a complete target-only Pinyin reading. Accepted Chinese 00–04
contribute 3,000 preserved cards/maps; delivered Chinese 00–05 totals **4,000**.
Submission and editorial `accepted` rows do not constitute ORCHESTRATOR acceptance.
The seven accepted Spanish stages and their 7,600 cards remain an unchanged project
dependency, not learner prerequisites or translation material.

This completes the assigned **part-A allocation** of shared guide §6.6. It does not
complete the joint 05/06 milestone. Stage 06 retains its distinct professional,
written, abstract and indirect-development **part B**, including its fuller formal
communication and feedback work. The brief written/spoken and rhetorical bridges
here support that later work without claiming to deliver it. This project split is
not a separate official syllabus or a CEFR equivalence; the final ten-stage Chinese
reconciliation remains later work.

## Allocation and actual hidden coverage

Counters in this introduction have prefix `zh-05-` unless another stage is stated.
Ranges below identify hidden retrieval, not every unfamiliar visible word.

| Block | Counters | Cards | Guide / role | Actual development |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| A | 0001–0220 | 220 | §6.6, §11 / P | Ordinary/social life, relationships, emotion, evaluation, description, narrative and nonfiction vocabulary |
| B | 0221–0380 | 160 | §6.6 / P | Precise event framing, extended and literal result/direction/degree/potential complements, repetition and temporal scope |
| C | 0381–0530 | 150 | §6.6 / P | Topic movement, reference, recoverable omission, text sequence and cohesive relations |
| D | 0531–0660 | 130 | §6.6 / P | Contemporary idioms and four-character expressions in actual collocations and bounded situations |
| E | 0661–0790 | 130 | §6.6 / P | Particles, stance, conversational repair, courtesy and ordinary disagreement with explicit interaction |
| F | 0791–0900 | 110 | §6.6 / P | Narrative/nonfiction relations, summaries, comparison and lexical reformulation with supplied source evidence |
| G | 0901–1000 | 100 | §6.6, §§8–13 / P | Additional ordinary lexical breadth and a limited contemporary written/spoken bridge |
| Total | 0001–1000 | 1,000 | P only; no R | Assigned part A |

**A — lexical breadth.** 0001–0040 develops concern, regret, relief, interpersonal
boundaries and appreciation through personally owned or explicitly attributed
responses; 0041–0080 develops reasoning, care, reminders, interference, bias,
criticism, teasing and praise. Near neighbors such as 惦记/牵挂/惦念, 内疚/愧疚,
照料/照应, 叮嘱/嘱咐, 打趣/嘲讽/讽刺 and 羡慕/嫉妒/眼红 have concrete local
frames rather than invented categorical synonym boundaries. 0081–0120 adds ways
of looking, posture, movement, vocal expression and visible demeanor without
turning an observation into a medical diagnosis or private-character claim.
0121–0160 supplies light, weather, landscape, plants, terrain and material change
in original descriptions. 0161–0200 broadens livelihood, household resources,
received versus planned amounts, social ties, craft, care obligations, neighborhood
life and belonging. 0201–0220 develops depiction, narrative setup/suspense,
aesthetic response, insight, ability and reflective learning. These are lexical
counterparts where prior concepts exist, not 220 newly discovered concepts.

**B — framing and complements.** 0221–0228 distinguishes remembered prior state,
an action being due or imminent, sustained present condition and earlier versus
just-achieved status; aspect is not a past-tense label. 0229–0280 develops actual
lexical combinations for understanding, recovery, endurance, restrained response,
emergence/penetration, omitted/included amounts, regained results, direction,
transmission, continuation and assembly. The visible events identify agency,
attempted versus achieved result and viewpoint. 0281–0310 retrieves potential
frames with particular interaction, capacity, affordability, tolerance, access,
social and practical limits: having a reason or desire is not automatically having
the capacity or opportunity. 0311–0320 focuses meaningful compact degree/result
components within visible predicates, including figurative intensity; the blank
does not hide the whole argument. 0321–0340 distinguishes repeated actual events,
concessive completed action, frequency, succession, abruptness, temporary interval
and uncertain/deferred future timing. 0341–0380 develops bounded waiting, leaving
behind, resource exhaustion, physical change and extended overthrow/exposure,
suppression, clearing, tension, replacement, allocation and conversion. Each
record retains its own realized, potential, negated or pending status rather than
claiming that an old morpheme with a new object is automatically new grammar.

**C — topic, omission and cohesion.** 0381–0396 retrieves topic introduction,
textual 照应 versus care-related 照应, response/transition, following an actual
thread, diversion and stopping. 0397–0404 distinguishes inserted earlier account,
reverse order, surrounding text reference and forward sequence from the supplied
connected material. 0405–0432 develops topic turns, asides, main business, deferred
matters, text organization, omitted wording and copied/reused wording; 两码事,
一码归一码 and 另当别论 preserve what is and is not being equated. 0433–0460 adds
temporary assumptions, addition/reversal/concession, summary, reference times,
event sequence and explicit antecedents. Omission is licensed by recoverable
actors and objects, not missing context. 0461–0478 retrieves people, closed groups,
selves, companions, parties and sides; 0468/0469 contrast traveling together with
the same trade through 同行's actual readings and roles. 0473/0474 locate the two
sides in actual exchanges; 0476–0478 point back to actual action/event/category.
0479–0500 preserves expected versus actual continuation, revised or pending
decisions, unchanged reproduction, alternatives, condition/result and the scope
of an overall assessment. 0501–0530 covers relevance, turn completion/interruption,
point-of-transition 就此, means/purpose 借此, endpoint 至此, compact class/set
reference, outcomes and text naming/abbreviation. Lexical counterparts and first
focused retrieval after earlier visible support are identified in each map.

**D — idioms in use.** 0531–0560 supplies understanding, visible agreement and
disagreement, speaking freely or hesitantly, indirect cues, implied meanings and
wordplay. The speaker's confirmation or actual explanatory evidence establishes
the intended interpretation; a gesture alone does not prove it. 0561–0570 covers
coherent, reasoned, concise, focused or empty talk. 0571–0586 develops practice,
transfer, progressive learning, persistence, abandonment and bounded completion
without claiming real learner attainment. 0587–0600 covers care, improvement,
carelessness, divided attention, concentration and orderly or flustered action.
0601–0621 develops perspective taking, considerate help, urgently needed help
versus enhancement, excessive additions, counterproductive results, actual
effort/output tradeoffs and appropriate resource use. 0622–0640 grounds surprise,
doubt, mistaken belief, vivid memory, attachment, mixed feelings and composure in
owned responses. 0641–0660 distinguishes anxiety and indecision, being at a loss,
lacking a workable plan or authority, resignation, adaptation, unforced
development, advance preparation versus prevention, plan-based confidence and
capacity/effort/income limits. Four-character forms are not all automatically
called chengyu: productive expressions and conventional four-character phrases
retain appropriate labels. Useful literal associations, contemporary frames and
register/unsuitable contexts are supplied; unnecessary origin stories are omitted.

**E — interaction and stance.** 0661–0680 supplies actual attention calls,
disappointed reaction, mistake/pain/surprise response, pointing, realization,
acknowledgment, puzzlement, quiet request, greeting, affirmative assent, standalone
呀/哇 versus earlier final particles, new-state 咯, letting a matter pass and
abandoning a proposal. The prior turn, relationship, actual intention and follow-up
are visible; imagined prosody does not repair missing stance. 0681–0706 develops
negotiability, manageability, measured disagreement, relevant/reliable assessment,
fallible likelihood, no-wonder realization, coincidence, counterexpectation and
rhetorical ignorance. 0707–0720 covers lengthy/mixed accounts, topic closure
versus degree emphasis, appreciative refusal, hospitality, privacy, indulgence,
candid disclosure, interpretation repair, diverted/omitted speech and turn
yielding. 0721–0740 develops task-sensitive closings, praise responses, closeness,
overinterpretation, concern, requests/thanks, victory courtesy and guidance.
0741–0760 distinguishes joking content, ridicule, literal belief, exactness,
contrary argument, retort and casual speech; 0753 retrieves service-courtesy 慢用
rather than repeating 0337's already focused 改天 invitation deferral. 0761–0790
develops rhetorical extent, allowing a choice, necessity/worth, purpose,
reservation, response management, acceptance/concession, refusal, turn pickup,
mediation, angry/reverse words and a face-saving exit. 0771 introduces 拿捏 as a
wording/degree-management verb after 0025's interpersonal 分寸, rather than adding
有 to repack the same objective. Courtesy does not prove sincerity, gratitude
does not imply acceptance, disagreement does not prove disrespect, and a single
exchange does not establish a permanent trait. Stage 06's full professional
feedback and indirect-development scope remains reserved.

**F — source-preserving narrative and reformulation.** 0791–0800 develops reason,
origin/course, whole sequence, hypothetical conception and inference. 0801–0823
adds reasoning/discussion, attributed reports and fictional oral tales, grounds,
summing up, causal attribution/credit/blame, start/end, dependence, contribution,
actual causal result, secondary action and subsequent development. 0824–0840
distinguishes event ending, overall termination, resolving one matter, phase
closure, interruption/stopping, action shift, viewpoint and bounded comparison.
0841–0861 covers equality/value/effect, intended reference, plain explanation,
crux, clarification/defense, recounting and personal/text genres, experience,
gist/outline/main point and connected development. 0846 introduces 症结 rather
than repeating 0493's 归根结底 through a spelling variant. 0862–0889 develops
contrastive portrayal, confirmed implication, relative emphasis, indirect
description, simile/metaphor/personification, exaggeration, repetition,
parallelism and paired phrasing through short original supplied lines and the
writer's stated interpretation. 0871 retrieves the noun 比方 in its own frame
after the earlier 打个比方 formula, not merely a shorter blank in that formula.
0890–0900 develops pursuing an explanation, resolving uncertainty, paired
reasons, concession, relative comparison and evidence-bearing examples. Each
summary or comparison includes the short original material actually being
reformulated; participants, quantities, time, attribution, conditions,
uncertainty, commitments and omissions are preserved. Cause is distinct from
evidence, sequence and purpose. Missing information is not proof of absence.
These are varied narrative/nonfiction and lexical counterparts, not an advanced
research or professional-writing course.

**G — breadth and the early bridge.** 0901–0912 supplies concise written/spoken
counterparts for gradual development, repeated acts, continuation to now,
concession, closed-set all-reference, hope, expectation, greeting and willing
acceptance. 0913–0930 develops bounded assessments of openness, stubbornness,
frankness, honesty, consideration, modesty, arrogance, bashfulness, fitness,
thoroughness and achievement. 0931–0942 covers owned ease, leisure, comfort,
settled condition, joy, wistfulness, physically cramped space and aesthetic or
functional object qualities. 0943–0962 broadens music, performance, visual art
and crafts through self-contained textual accounts, distinct participants,
materials and processes. 0963–0982 adds trade/know-how, method, interpretation,
wording deliberation, plan development, examination, page consultation versus
complete/selected/skim reading, signs, leads and settled arrangements.
0983–1000 develops communication/whereabouts/destination, an object's settled
home, invented or personally attributed local customs, landscape/atmosphere,
attachment, old-fashioned style versus age, traces versus site, teaching a
skill, home-region attachment and standard words for hometown speech/regional
variety. No dialect production, universal custom, historical authenticity,
medical outcome or attained skill is inferred from these accounts.

## Progression, references and review limits

The 3,000-card accepted hidden inventory and relevant complete predecessor
records/maps informed every batch; already ingested 05 inventory and actual
relevant contexts were supplied before each fresh author/editor pair. Maps name
actual prerequisite contributions or introduce recoverable support. Same strings
can serve distinct senses, frames or functions, and honest new lexical
counterparts can share a known concept. Conversely, accidental substrings such as
留言中 + 断言, 门 + 道谢, 手指 + 点 or 昨天 + 知道 are not prior lessons in 中断,
门道, 指点 or 天知道. Earlier visible support is distinguished from focused
retrieval rather than erased to claim novelty.

Readings cover the complete actual hidden unit, with lexical tones and supported
neutral syllables; useful sandhi is separate. References establish particular
whole readings or uses, never supply the source sentences. The retained primary
decisions distinguish actual indexed excerpts, opened passages and failed reads:

- 0461's 人家 is pronoun rénjia, distinct from household rénjiā; 0468/0469 use
  tóngxíng/tóngháng for travel/trade. 0473 selects whole-side zhètou from actual
  [MOE](https://dict.revised.moe.edu.tw/dictView.jsp?ID=115040) and Trainchinese
  evidence; 0474 selects nàtóu in the actual phone-end use. 0456 起头 qǐtóu,
  0396 话头 huàtóu, 0453 末了 mòliǎo and 0530 那会儿 nàhuìr retain whole-unit
  decisions; an unrelated animal classifier or character default is insufficient.
- 0488 如法炮制 selects páo from the
  [whole expression](https://dict.concised.moe.edu.tw/dictView.jsp?ID=36315&la=0&powerMode=0).
  0499 切入 qiērù differs from 0500 切中 qièzhòng and 0501 切题 qiètí.
  0557 话里有话 selects lexical huà lǐ yǒu huà from
  [whole-word evidence](https://dict.revised.moe.edu.tw/dictView.jsp?ID=84025);
  a source's connected-speech tone is not silently made a lexical tone.
- 0549 吞吞吐吐, 0550 支支吾吾, 0551 含糊其辞, 0566 言归正传,
  0589 粗心大意 and 0590 马马虎虎 retain scoped whole-expression readings.
  Full hú/dàyì in selected fixed expressions does not invalidate supported weak
  standalone forms. 0598 从容不迫 selects Putonghua cóngróng from actual
  Trainchinese whole evidence and an educator publication; the differing regional
  cōng head is not silently imposed. 0612 适得其反 and 0635 哭笑不得 retain
  lexical dé, 0636 忍俊不禁 jīn, 0638 闷闷不乐 mèn; 0658–0660 retain actual
  whole liàng/xíng/jìn/wéi choices.
- Actually opened Collins [哦](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/哦)
  and [哎哟](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/哎哟)
  support ò realization and whole āiyō. 哎呀 full/weak variants, 嗨 hāi with
  documented hēi variant, 噢 ō acknowledgment, indicating 喏 nuò and neutral
  咯 lo use actual whole meaningful words/utterances. 0674's affirmative ǹg
  combines actual whole Trainchinese affirmative evidence with
  [Unicode transcription evidence](https://www.unicode.org/L2/L2023/23269-kmandarin-updates.pdf)
  and a primary interactional study; the function is also explicit in the front.
  Garbled indexed tone marks do not independently verify a reading.
- 0678 selects bàliǎo letting a matter pass, distinct from accepted restrictive
  bàle; 0685 中肯 selects zhòngkěn; 0688 够呛 gòuqiàng and 0698 敢情 gǎnqing
  retain actual whole-source decisions. 0692's 保不齐 bǎobùqí uses the
  [whole MOE head](https://dict.revised.moe.edu.tw/dictView.jsp?ID=15625&q=1&word=342)
  and a primary linguistic account of possibility, not a universal ban on weak
  variants. 0697's guàibude is directly printed in the opened
  [Collins whole head](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/怪不得);
  MOE's full-bù variant remains supported too.
- For 0693 说不准, earlier targeted dictionary searches and direct MDBG,
  CC-CEDICT/ChinesePod reads did not produce accessible complete primary Pinyin.
  The actual indexed [北辞郎 entry](https://www.ctrans.org/search.php?opts=ex&word=说不)
  displays whole shuō bù zhǔn and the cannot-say-clearly meaning; the selected
  form was also reviewed in its actual utterance. The direct exact-target read
  was inaccessible. Cambridge's indexed head supports uncertain-answer meaning,
  not Pinyin; its direct read failed403. These limits are not claimed as opened
  verification or a categorical rejection of neutral bu.
- 0726 哪里哪里, 0735 劳烦, 0750 还嘴 and 0753 慢用 use actual whole courtesy or
  response evidence. 0771 拿捏 selects weak nánie in degree/wording management
  from the [whole MOE entry](https://dict.revised.moe.edu.tw/dictView.jsp?ID=56096&la=0&powerMode=0),
  not its distinct control-person sense. 0784 接茬 uses actual jiēchá from
  [Han Dian's whole entry](https://zdic.net/hans/接茬); a publisher's chá
  annotation alone does not verify all syllables or conversational meaning.
- 0827 了结 liǎojié and 0858 gist 大意 dàyì use whole Collins evidence, distinct
  from careless dàyi. 0846 症结 zhēngjié uses the
  [whole Collins head](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/症结)
  and MOE's corresponding figurative crux entry, without a medical claim.
- 0915 执拗 zhíniù, 0963 行当 hángdang, 0965 门道 méndao, 0968 揣摩 chuǎimó
  and 0982 着落 zhuóluò retain whole-source choices and supported full/weak variants.
  0951 selects huàjuàn for 画卷 using the actual
  [HKU Press preview](https://hkupress.hku.hk/image/catalog/pdf-preview/9789888139200.pdf)
  printed p94/PDF19 and an independent educational whole-word list. The opened
  extraction retains huàjuàn but adjacent Chinese is garbled; indexed publisher
  text aligns the whole word. The attempted PDF screenshot was unavailable, so
  visual inspection is not claimed. Actual whole huàjuǎn sources are recorded as
  discrepant variants, not falsely described as character-default errors.

Except for passages explicitly described as opened, these decisions rely on
actual indexed primary excerpts and editorial assessment, with their scope
retained. A bibliography does not verify every word. Failed/gated access was not
bypassed; no examples, bulk wordlists, historical stories or current examination
rules were imported. No Firecrawl, phonetic converter, installation or listening
was used. Sol 6.1 authoring, separate editing and the exhaustive final pass are
model-assisted editorial review; no independent native-human review is claimed.
The PR records actual process settings, ranges, findings, checks, final hashes
and exact-head CI. Machine checks establish source integrity, not naturalness,
Pinyin correctness, lexical novelty or proficiency.

## Reproducible source-string measures and handover

| Source-string measure | Chinese 05 | Chinese 00–05 cumulative |
| --- | ---: | ---: |
| Cards / mappings | 1,000 | 4,000 |
| Exact target strings | 997 | 3,737 |
| Exact lemma strings | 997 | 3,731 |
| Operational (lemma, meaning) pairs | 1,000 | 3,999 |
| D: direct-target Han | 1,012 | 1,675 |
| S: whole-sentence Han | 1,771 | 2,022 |
| V: visible-front Han | 1,452 | 1,629 |
| I: incidental-only Han (S − D) | 759 | 347 |

Methods use NFC exact strings: targets are distinct `cloze` strings, lemmas are
distinct `lemma` strings, and operational pairs are exact (`lemma`, `meaning`)
tuples, including the complete authored English gloss. These are reproducible
source measures, not semantic word segmentation, exhaustive lexicon counts or
distinct mastered meanings. Same target strings may legitimately retrieve
different contextual functions, and different strings may share a concept.

Han sets use only U+3400–U+4DBF and U+4E00–U+9FFF. **D** is the union of Han in
hidden clozes; **S** is the union in complete sentences; **V** is the union after
removing each card's own single cloze occurrence from its sentence, once; **I**
is S minus D, the incidental-only set. V can overlap D because a character
hidden on one card can be visible on another. Back-side notes, Pinyin and English
are excluded. Stage columns use only 05; cumulative columns use actual unchanged
00–04 plus 05. Raw occurrence does not establish recognition or handwriting.

The shared Band 5–6 **working landmarks** remain at least **1,800 recognized
characters**, approximately **5,500–7,000 words/phrases** and approximately **700
handwritten characters**. These remain separate from both card counts and the
guide's cited standard anchor, including Band 6's 1,800 characters/5,456
words/phrases. The pinned guide retains its
[standard reference](https://wuzhou.hfbook.cn/api/wzsc/arc/arc/file/CA1635406039455178/81630125/AT1665387053573178.pdf);
no newly verified examination inventory is claimed. Typed completion, incidental
Han occurrence and source-string coverage do not prove handwriting, speaking,
listening or attained vocabulary. Stage 06 must deliver its own professional,
written, abstract and indirect part B before the shared milestone can be
reconciled as complete; later Chinese stages and the final language-wide
reconciliation remain unimplemented here.

| zh-05-0002 | A | 牵挂 retrieves continuing concern for an absent family member despite regular reassuring contact; the feeling belongs explicitly to the speaker. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | A parent describes concern for a daughter studying away from home. | P | zh-05-0001 supplies concern for an absent traveler through 惦记; adds 牵挂 and recoverable support 报平安. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0003 | A | 惦念 adds a lexical counterpart for affectionate thought about an absent person, supported by continuing holiday contact rather than a new concern concept. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Keeping in touch with a former woodworking teacher who moved away. | P | zh-05-0001 retrieves 惦记 for keeping someone in mind; introduces 惦念 and explains 木工 and the stated teaching relationship. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0004 | A | 眷恋 retrieves deep attachment to a long-term home and reluctance to leave despite an actual moving decision, rather than simple recollection. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Reflecting on leaving a city after twenty years. | P | Familiar places and explicit 舍不得离开 make the new attachment verb recoverable; adds a lexical expression beyond recalling or knowing a place. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0005 | A | 惋惜 adds regret over a missed valued opportunity, with the exhibition already ended and no assertion that the speaker caused the loss. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Missing a photography exhibition because free time came too late. | P | The explicit yesterday-versus-today timing supports the missed opportunity; introduces a lexical regret counterpart to existing regret vocabulary. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0006 | A | 懊悔 retrieves regret directed at the speaker's own declined opportunity, with both the earlier choice and later reason for regret explicit. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Regretting a declined invitation after hearing it was a friend's final joint performance with another actor. | P | zh-05-0005 supplies missed-opportunity regret through 惋惜; adds self-directed 懊悔 and explains 同台. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0007 | A | 内疚 adds self-reported guilt about an unfulfilled promise affecting a colleague, contrasting with feeling unfairly treated rather than implying the same emotional direction. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Admitting forgetting an agreed food-storage favor. | P | zh-04-0841 retrieves 委屈 for perceived unfair treatment; adds 内疚 for the speaker's acknowledged responsibility and resulting guilt. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0008 | A | Verbal 冤枉 retrieves wrongly blaming a person when the speaker explicitly identifies themself as the responsible actor, extending beyond naming the affected person's hurt. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Correcting blame for a door left unlocked. | P | zh-04-0841 supplies the affected person's feeling through 委屈; introduces the transitive wrongful-blame verb with explicit contrary facts. | lexical | accepted | none |
| zh-05-0009 | A | 憋屈 adds colloquial stifled grievance with both an attributed wrong and blocked opportunity to explain visible, rather than merely a new gloss for sadness. | [Guide](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#66-advanced-development) | Feeling unable to challenge a colleague's claim to one's proposal during a meeting. | P | zh-04-0841 retrieves 委屈 for perceived unfair treatment; adds 憋屈 with the explicitly blocked explanation supporting its stifled quality. | lexical | accepted | none |
